```python
            all(clear > 0.0 for _, clear in guard_clearances),
            f"all {len(guard_clearances)} structures under the readout clear it (binding: {binding[0]} +{binding[1]:.1f}dp)",
        )

        ndc_left, _ = project(FAR_LEFT_EDGE)
        ndc_right, _ = project(STAIR_FACADE_RIGHT)
        left_frac = (ndc_left + 1.0) / 2.0
        right_frac = (ndc_right + 1.0) / 2.0
        check(
            0.0 <= left_frac and right_frac <= 0.62,
            f"whole Persepolis facade in frame on the left (spans {left_frac:.2f}..{right_frac:.2f} of width)",
        )

        corner_fracs = [(project(c)[0] + 1.0) / 2.0 for c in PLATFORM_CORNERS]
        exact_platform_right = max(corner_fracs) * w
        check(
            max(corner_fracs) <= 0.70,
            f"cut-back platform ends before the open right landscape (right edge at {max(corner_fracs):.2f} of width)",
        )

        # Exact Mil tower/slope placement against PersepolisFraming's fields.
        tower_ndc, tower_row = project(MIL_TOWER_TOP)
        tower_x = (tower_ndc + 1.0) / 2.0 * w
        err_tower_x = abs(tower_x - f["mil_tower_center_x"]) / dp
        err_tower_top = abs(tower_row - f["mil_tower_top_y"]) / dp
        check(
            max(err_tower_x, err_tower_top) < 1.0,
            "Mil tower view-space landmarks match exact projection "
            f"(centre x {tower_x / w:.3f} of width, top {tower_row / h:.3f} of scene; "
            f"errors {err_tower_x:.2f}/{err_tower_top:.2f}dp)",
        )
        check(
            0.0 < tower_x < w and tower_row > 0.36 * h,
            "Mil tower is in frame and clear of the header band",
        )

        slope_projected = [project(c) for c in MIL_SLOPE_CORNERS]
        exact_slope_left = min((ndc + 1.0) / 2.0 * w for ndc, _ in slope_projected)
        exact_slope_right = max((ndc + 1.0) / 2.0 * w for ndc, _ in slope_projected)
        exact_slope_top = min(row for _, row in slope_projected)
        exact_slope_front = max(row for _, row in slope_projected)
        slope_errors = [
            abs(exact_slope_left - f["mil_slope_left_x"]) / dp,
            abs(exact_slope_right - f["mil_slope_right_x"]) / dp,
            abs(exact_slope_top - f["mil_slope_top_y"]) / dp,
            abs(exact_slope_front - f["mil_slope_front_y"]) / dp,
        ]
        check(
            max(slope_errors) < 1.0,
            "Mil slope footprint landmarks match its transformed fixed sheets "
            f"(x {exact_slope_left / w:.3f}..{exact_slope_right / w:.3f} of width, "
            f"rows {exact_slope_top / h:.3f}..{exact_slope_front / h:.3f}; "
            f"max error {max(slope_errors):.2f}dp)",
        )
        phone_window = f["visible_width_m"] <= 180.0
        check(
            tower_x >= 0.58 * w
            and exact_slope_left > exact_platform_right + 0.10 * w
            and (
                not phone_window
                or (tower_x >= 0.89 * w and exact_slope_right >= 1.03 * w)
            ),
            "Mil stands alone at the far right with open plain from Persepolis "
            f"(platform {exact_platform_right / w:.2f}, slope {exact_slope_left / w:.2f}.."
            f"{exact_slope_right / w:.2f}; phone slope is cropped by the edge)"
            if phone_window else
            "Mil stands alone to the right with open plain from Persepolis "
            f"(platform {exact_platform_right / w:.2f}, slope {exact_slope_left / w:.2f}.."
            f"{exact_slope_right / w:.2f})",
        )
        check(
            f["toe_y"] < exact_slope_front <= f["toe_y"] + 0.027 * h,
            "slope front is nearer than the Persepolis toe but inside its contact-shadow band "
            f"({(exact_slope_front - f['toe_y']) / dp:+.1f}dp from toe row)",
        )

        _, fore_row = project(FORECOURT_FRONT_TOP)
        band_lo, band_hi = f["toe_y"] - 0.032 * h, f["toe_y"] + 0.030 * h
        check(
            band_lo <= fore_row <= band_hi,
            f"forecourt front edge inside the painted grounding bands ({(fore_row - f['toe_y']) / dp:+.1f}dp from toe row)",
        )

        # Approximation quality for the Persepolis landmarks (generous bound):
        err_col = abs(col_row - f["column_top_y"]) / dp
        err_door = abs(door_row - f["doorway_top_y"]) / dp
        err_plat = abs(platform_row - f["platform_top_y"]) / dp
        err_edge = abs(exact_platform_right - f["platform_right_x"]) / dp
        check(
            max(err_col, err_door, err_plat, err_edge) < 8.0,
            f"Persepolis isotropic-scale approximation error < 8dp (column {err_col:.1f}, doorway {err_door:.1f}, platform {err_plat:.1f}, platform edge {err_edge:.1f})",
        )

    print("\n" + ("ALL CHECKS PASSED" if not failures else f"{len(failures)} FAILURE(S)"))
    return 1 if failures else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

# 6 — Landscape tests

## 21. `app/src/test/java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy/ui/rendering/LandscapeProfileTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.alijafari.red.astronomy.ui.rendering

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import kotlin.math.abs

/**
 * Contracts for the photographic landscape's profile math: every value bounded, every
 * skyline deterministic (the ground must never shimmer between clock ticks), envelopes
 * honouring their control points, and mountain heights forming a continuous crested wall
 * rather than noise soup.
 */
class LandscapeProfileTest {

    @Test
    fun clusterPolygonIsDeterministicIrregularAndBounded() {
        // The anti-blob contract for leaf clusters: same seed -> same outline,
        // radii bounded around r (never a perfect circle), and the perimeter
        // genuinely irregular (max/min radius ratio well above 1 for most seeds).
        var irregular = 0
        for (seed in listOf(3, 17, 101, 555, 909, 4242)) {
            for (idx in 1..12) {
                val a = LandscapeProfile.clusterPolygon(10f, 0.78f, seed, idx)
                val b = LandscapeProfile.clusterPolygon(10f, 0.78f, seed, idx)
                assertEquals("cluster size", 14, a.size)
                for (k in a.indices) assertEquals("deterministic", a[k], b[k], 0f)
                var rMin = Float.MAX_VALUE
                var rMax = 0f
                var k = 0
                while (k < a.size) {
                    val r = kotlin.math.sqrt(a[k] * a[k] + a[k + 1] * a[k + 1] / 0.78f / 0.78f)
                    assertTrue("cluster radius bounded ($r)", r in 4.5f..12.5f)
                    if (r < rMin) rMin = r
                    if (r > rMax) rMax = r
                    k += 2
                }
                if (rMax / rMin > 1.25f) irregular++
            }
        }
        assertTrue("most clusters irregular ($irregular/72)", irregular > 48)
        // Different seeds must give genuinely different outlines (variation is
        // structural, not a re-rotation of one shape).
        var distinct = 0
        for (idx in 1..12) {
            val a = LandscapeProfile.clusterPolygon(10f, 0.78f, 101, idx)
            val b = LandscapeProfile.clusterPolygon(10f, 0.78f, 202, idx)
            var maxDiff = 0f
            for (k in a.indices) maxDiff = maxOf(maxDiff, abs(a[k] - b[k]))
            if (maxDiff > 1.5f) distinct++
        }
        assertTrue("seed variation is structural ($distinct/12)", distinct > 8)
    }

    @Test
    fun canopyOutlineIsDeterministicAndHugsItsAnchors() {
        // Anchors: an asymmetric ring of branch tips around a crown centre.
        val pts = ArrayList<Float>()
        for (i in 0 until 40) {
            val a = i / 40f * 2f * Math.PI.toFloat()
            val r = 0.30f + 0.18f * kotlin.math.sin(a * 2f) * kotlin.math.cos(a * 3f) +
                0.05f * LandscapeProfile.hash01(77, i)
            pts.add(kotlin.math.cos(a) * r)
            pts.add(0.65f + kotlin.math.sin(a) * r * 0.8f)
        }
        val arr = pts.toFloatArray()
        val o1 = LandscapeProfile.canopyOutline(arr, 0f, 0.65f, 42)
        val o2 = LandscapeProfile.canopyOutline(arr, 0f, 0.65f, 42)
        assertEquals("14 sectors x,y", 28, o1.size)
        for (k in o1.indices) assertEquals("deterministic", o1[k], o2[k], 0f)
        var rMin = Float.MAX_VALUE
        var rMax = 0f
        var k = 0
        while (k < o1.size) {
            val dx = o1[k]
            val dy = (o1[k + 1] - 0.65f) / 0.8f
            val r = kotlin.math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
            assertTrue("outline hugs anchors ($r)", r in 0.08f..0.66f)
            if (r < rMin) rMin = r
            if (r > rMax) rMax = r
            k += 2
        }
        assertTrue("outline is asymmetric ($rMax/$rMin)", rMax / rMin > 1.15f)
    }

    @Test
    fun hashAndNoiseStayInRangeAndAreDeterministic() {
        for (seed in listOf(0, 3, 11, 29, 317, -7)) {
            for (i in -50..500) {
                val h = LandscapeProfile.hash01(seed, i)
                assertTrue("hash01 in 0..1 (seed=$seed i=$i): $h", h in 0f..1f)
                assertEquals(h, LandscapeProfile.hash01(seed, i), 0f)
            }
            var x = -4f
            while (x < 40f) {
                val n = LandscapeProfile.valueNoise(x, seed)
                assertTrue("valueNoise in -1..1 (seed=$seed x=$x): $n", n in -1f..1f)
                assertEquals(n, LandscapeProfile.valueNoise(x, seed), 0f)
                val f = LandscapeProfile.fbm(x * 0.31f, seed)
                assertTrue("fbm in -1..1 (seed=$seed x=$x): $f", f in -1f..1f)
                x += 0.37f
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun valueNoiseIsContinuousAcrossLatticePoints() {
        for (k in -3..12) {
            val before = LandscapeProfile.valueNoise(k - 1e-4f, 11)
            val at = LandscapeProfile.valueNoise(k.toFloat(), 11)
            assertTrue("continuous at lattice $k", abs(at - before) < 0.01f)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun ridgedStaysInUnitRangeAndReachesCrestedPeaks() {
        var maxSeen = 0f
        var p = 0f
        while (p <= 1f) {
            val r = LandscapeProfile.ridged(p, seed = 29)
            assertTrue("ridged in 0..1 (p=$p): $r", r in 0f..1f)
            assertEquals(r, LandscapeProfile.ridged(p, seed = 29), 0f)
            if (r > maxSeen) maxSeen = r
            p += 0.004f
        }
        assertTrue("ridged actually crests (max=$maxSeen)", maxSeen > 0.55f)
    }

    @Test
    fun envelopeHitsControlPointsAndHoldsItsEnds() {
        val pts = floatArrayOf(0f, 0.3f, 0.4f, 0.9f, 1f, 0.5f)
        assertEquals(0.3f, LandscapeProfile.envelope(-0.2f, pts), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(0.3f, LandscapeProfile.envelope(0f, pts), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(0.9f, LandscapeProfile.envelope(0.4f, pts), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(0.5f, LandscapeProfile.envelope(1f, pts), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(0.5f, LandscapeProfile.envelope(1.7f, pts), 1e-4f)
        // Smoothstep segments are monotonic between their control points.
        var prev = LandscapeProfile.envelope(0f, pts)
        var p = 0.02f
        while (p <= 0.4f) {
            val v = LandscapeProfile.envelope(p, pts)
            assertTrue("rising segment monotonic at $p", v >= prev - 1e-6f)
            prev = v
            p += 0.02f
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun mountainHeightsFormAContinuousWallWithinTheEnvelope() {
        val envelope = floatArrayOf(0f, 0.3f, 0.6f, 0.8f, 0.8f, 1f, 1f, 0.7f)
        val heights = LandscapeProfile.mountainHeights(140, 240f, envelope, seed = 29)
        assertEquals(141, heights.size)
        for (i in heights.indices) {
            val p = i / 140f
            val ceiling = 240f * LandscapeProfile.envelope(p, envelope)
            assertTrue("height positive at $i", heights[i] > 0f)
            assertTrue(
                "height under envelope ceiling at $i (${heights[i]} <= $ceiling)",
                heights[i] <= ceiling + 0.01f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "wall never collapses to the plain at $i",
                heights[i] >= ceiling * 0.40f - 0.01f
            )
        }
        // The crest at the envelope's peak must clear the shoulder decisively.
        val shoulder = heights.copyOfRange(0, 20).max()
        val crest = heights.copyOfRange(100, 125).max()
        assertTrue("crest ($crest) towers over shoulder ($shoulder)", crest > shoulder * 1.5f)
        // Height sampling agrees with the array at its nodes.
        assertEquals(heights[70], LandscapeProfile.heightAt(heights, 0.5f), 0.01f)
        assertEquals(heights.first(), LandscapeProfile.heightAt(heights, -1f), 0.01f)
        assertEquals(heights.last(), LandscapeProfile.heightAt(heights, 2f), 0.01f)
    }

    @Test
    fun benchesTerraceAndNotchesCarveWithoutBreakingBounds() {
        val envelope = floatArrayOf(0f, 0.6f, 0.5f, 1f, 1f, 0.8f)
        val plain = LandscapeProfile.mountainHeights(140, 240f, envelope, seed = 29)
        val benched = LandscapeProfile.mountainHeights(
            140, 240f, envelope, seed = 29, benchAmp = 0.5f, benchStep = 0.14f
        )
        assertTrue(benched.all { it in 0f..240f })
        // Terracing measurably changes the profile but never by more than a bench step.
        var changed = 0
        for (i in plain.indices) {
            val d = abs(benched[i] - plain[i])
            assertTrue("bench shift bounded at $i: $d", d <= 240f * 0.14f)
            if (d > 0.5f) changed++
        }
        assertTrue("benches actually bite ($changed samples)", changed > 20)

        val notched = LandscapeProfile.mountainHeights(
            140, 240f, envelope, seed = 29,
            notches = floatArrayOf(0.5f, 0.03f, 0.4f)
        )
        val atNotch = notched[70]
        assertTrue("notch carves the crest ($atNotch vs ${plain[70]})", atNotch < plain[70] * 0.75f)
        // …and heals away from the water-gap.
        assertEquals(plain[10], notched[10], plain[10] * 0.02f + 0.01f)
        assertTrue(notched.all { it >= 0f })
    }

    @Test
    fun treeSkeletonsAreDeterministicBoundedAndPipeShaped() {
        val a = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(seed = 101, aspect = 1.3f, trunkFraction = 0.36f, attractorCount = 90)
        val b = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(seed = 101, aspect = 1.3f, trunkFraction = 0.36f, attractorCount = 90)
        assertEquals("same seed, same tree", a, b)
        val other = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(seed = 202, aspect = 1.3f, trunkFraction = 0.36f, attractorCount = 90)
        assertTrue("different seed, different tree", a != other)

        assertTrue("skeleton has substance (${a.size} segments)", a.size in 40..440)
        assertTrue("terminals exist for foliage", a.count { it.terminal } >= 15)
        for (s in a) {
            assertTrue("x within crown (${s.x1})", abs(s.x1) <= 1.3f * 0.75f)
            assertTrue("y within tree (${s.y1})", s.y1 in -0.01f..1.06f)
            assertTrue("radii positive", s.r0 > 0f && s.r1 > 0f)
            assertTrue("child never fatter than parent (pipe model)", s.r1 <= s.r0 * 1.06f + 0.001f)
        }
        val trunk = a.first()
        assertEquals(0f, trunk.x0, 0f)
        assertEquals(0f, trunk.y0, 0f)
        assertTrue("trunk base is the stoutest segment", a.all { it.r0 <= trunk.r0 + 1e-4f })
        val crownTop = a.maxOf { it.y1 }
        assertTrue("crown reaches up ($crownTop)", crownTop > 0.8f)
        val spread = a.maxOf { it.x1 } - a.minOf { it.x1 }
        assertTrue("crown spreads ($spread)", spread > 0.7f)
    }

    @Test
    fun speciesSkeletonsSelfOrganizeDeterministically() {
        // Defaults are exactly the original pistachio recipe (guards drift).
        val plain = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(seed = 101, aspect = 1.3f, trunkFraction = 0.36f, attractorCount = 90)
        val explicit = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 101, aspect = 1.3f, trunkFraction = 0.36f, attractorCount = 90,
            tropism = 0.20f, vase = 0f, scaffoldSpread = 1f, arch = 0f, gnarl = 0f,
            occupancy = 0f, selfPrune = 0f, shellBias = 0.65f, topFlatten = 0.82f
        )
        assertEquals("defaults preserve the original growth", plain, explicit)

        // Self-pruning sheds a strict subset of the same grown tree.
        val unpruned = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(seed = 707, aspect = 1.75f, trunkFraction = 0.30f, attractorCount = 115)
        val pruned = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 707, aspect = 1.75f, trunkFraction = 0.30f, attractorCount = 115, selfPrune = 0.6f
        )
        assertTrue("pruning sheds wood (${pruned.size} < ${unpruned.size})", pruned.size < unpruned.size)
        val grown = unpruned.map { listOf(it.x0, it.y0, it.x1, it.y1) }.toSet()
        assertTrue(
            "pruned skeleton is a subset of the grown one",
            pruned.all { listOf(it.x0, it.y0, it.x1, it.y1) in grown }
        )

        // Oak recipe: broad decurrent spread, still pipe-shaped and bounded.
        val oak = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 707, aspect = 1.75f, trunkFraction = 0.30f, attractorCount = 115,
            scaffoldSpread = 1.30f, arch = 0.5f, gnarl = 0.55f, occupancy = 0.8f,
            selfPrune = 0.5f, shellBias = 0.80f, topFlatten = 0.78f
        )
        assertEquals("oak deterministic", oak, LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 707, aspect = 1.75f, trunkFraction = 0.30f, attractorCount = 115,
            scaffoldSpread = 1.30f, arch = 0.5f, gnarl = 0.55f, occupancy = 0.8f,
            selfPrune = 0.5f, shellBias = 0.80f, topFlatten = 0.78f
        ))
        assertTrue("oak has substance (${oak.size})", oak.size in 30..440)
        val oakSpread = oak.maxOf { it.x1 } - oak.minOf { it.x1 }
        assertTrue("oak spreads wider than it is tall ($oakSpread)", oakSpread > 1.0f)
        for (s in oak) {
            assertTrue("oak pipe model holds", s.r1 <= s.r0 * 1.06f + 0.001f)
            assertTrue("oak y in tree (${s.y1})", s.y1 in -0.02f..1.06f)
        }

        // Cherry recipe: the vase — crown terminals spread wider near the top of the
        // crown than near its foot.
        val cherry = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 909, aspect = 1.45f, trunkFraction = 0.36f, attractorCount = 125,
            vase = 0.55f, scaffoldSpread = 0.60f, arch = 0.8f, gnarl = 0.15f,
            occupancy = 0.6f, selfPrune = 0.25f, shellBias = 0.35f, topFlatten = 0.95f
        )
        assertTrue("cherry has substance (${cherry.size})", cherry.size in 30..440)
        val terminals = cherry.filter { it.terminal }
        // Blossom clusters also sit on depth>=6 segments, so terminals need only be
        // plentiful, not numerous — this seed grows ~14.
        assertTrue("cherry has terminals for blossom (${terminals.size})", terminals.size >= 10)
        val low = terminals.filter { it.y1 < 0.55f }
        val high = terminals.filter { it.y1 > 0.75f }
        if (low.size >= 3 && high.size >= 3) {
            val lowSpread = low.maxOf { it.x1 } - low.minOf { it.x1 }
            val highSpread = high.maxOf { it.x1 } - high.minOf { it.x1 }
            assertTrue("vase flares upward ($highSpread > $lowSpread)", highSpread > lowSpread)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun paulowniaSkeletonIsTallUmbrellaAndDeterministic() {
        // Paulownia recipe (SceneGroundRenderer.PAULOWNIA_SKELETONS): long clear
        // trunk, broad rounded-umbrella crown, half-open shell for the huge leaves.
        val paul = LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 1111, aspect = 1.08f, trunkFraction = 0.42f, attractorCount = 120,
            scaffoldSpread = 0.95f, arch = 0.35f, gnarl = 0.25f, occupancy = 0.65f,
            selfPrune = 0.35f, shellBias = 0.60f, topFlatten = 0.80f
        )
        assertEquals("Paulownia deterministic", paul, LandscapeProfile.treeSkeleton(
            seed = 1111, aspect = 1.08f, trunkFraction = 0.42f, attractorCount = 120,
            scaffoldSpread = 0.95f, arch = 0.35f, gnarl = 0.25f, occupancy = 0.65f,
            selfPrune = 0.35f, shellBias = 0.60f, topFlatten = 0.80f
        ))
        assertTrue("Paulownia has substance (${paul.size})", paul.size in 30..440)
        assertTrue("terminals exist for panicles/leaves", paul.count { it.terminal } >= 8)
        val crownTop = paul.maxOf { it.y1 }
        assertTrue("crown reaches up ($crownTop)", crownTop > 0.75f)
        val spread = paul.maxOf { it.x1 } - paul.minOf { it.x1 }
        assertTrue("umbrella crown spreads ($spread)", spread > 0.45f)
        for (seg in paul) {
            assertTrue("Paulownia pipe model holds", seg.r1 <= seg.r0 * 1.06f + 0.001f)
            assertTrue("Paulownia y in tree (${seg.y1})", seg.y1 in -0.02f..1.06f)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun grassClumpsAreDeterministicSharedAndBounded() {
        val a = LandscapeProfile.grassClump(0.30f, 0.62f, seed = 517)
        assertEquals(a, LandscapeProfile.grassClump(0.30f, 0.62f, seed = 517))
        // Two blades a hair apart almost always share their clump's traits exactly —
        // that patchiness is the entire point (measured over many sample pairs).
        var shared = 0
        val pairs = 120
        for (i in 0 until pairs) {
            val x = (i * 37 % 97) / 97f + 0.01f
            val d = (i * 53 % 89) / 89f + 0.01f
            val p = LandscapeProfile.grassClump(x, d, seed = 517)
            val q = LandscapeProfile.grassClump(x + 0.004f, d + 0.004f, seed = 517)
            if (p.heightMul == q.heightMul && p.lean == q.lean) shared++
            assertTrue("heightMul bounded", p.heightMul in 0.7f..1.35f)
            assertTrue("lean bounded", p.lean in -0.55f..0.55f)
            assertTrue("dryness bounded", p.dryness01 in 0f..1f)
            assertTrue("density bounded", p.density01 in 0f..1f)
            assertTrue("edge bounded", p.edge01 in 0f..1f)
        }
        assertTrue("neighbours usually share a clump ($shared/$pairs)", shared > pairs * 2 / 3)
        // And distant patches genuinely differ somewhere.
        val far = LandscapeProfile.grassClump(0.85f, 0.20f, seed = 517)
        assertTrue("clumps vary across the plain", far != a)
    }

    @Test
    fun bladeBendIsMonotonicRootedAndTipExact() {
        for (stiffness in listOf(0f, 0.35f, 0.7f, 1f)) {
            assertEquals(0f, LandscapeProfile.bladeBendX(0f, stiffness), 1e-6f)
            assertEquals(1f, LandscapeProfile.bladeBendX(1f, stiffness), 1e-6f)
            var prev = 0f
            var t = 0.05f
            while (t <= 1.001f) {
                val v = LandscapeProfile.bladeBendX(t.coerceAtMost(1f), stiffness)
                assertTrue("bend monotonic (s=$stiffness t=$t)", v >= prev - 1e-6f)
                assertTrue("bend bounded (s=$stiffness t=$t)", v in 0f..1f)
                prev = v
                t += 0.05f
            }
        }
        // A stiff blade has bent further by mid-height than a supple one.
        assertTrue(
            LandscapeProfile.bladeBendX(0.5f, 1f) > LandscapeProfile.bladeBendX(0.5f, 0f)
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun bladeWidthIsFullAtRootNeedleAtTipAndMonotonic() {
        assertEquals(1f, LandscapeProfile.bladeWidthProfile(0f), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(0f, LandscapeProfile.bladeWidthProfile(1f), 1e-6f)
        var prev = 1f
        var t = 0.05f
        while (t <= 1.001f) {
            val v = LandscapeProfile.bladeWidthProfile(t.coerceAtMost(1f))
            assertTrue("width bounded (t=$t)", v in 0f..1f)
            assertTrue("width narrows monotonically (t=$t)", v <= prev + 1e-6f)
            prev = v
            t += 0.05f
        }
        // Convex taper: most of the width is gone well before the tip (needle, not ribbon).
        assertTrue(LandscapeProfile.bladeWidthProfile(0.75f) < 0.35f)
        // Out-of-range heights clamp to the ends instead of going negative/NaN.
        assertEquals(1f, LandscapeProfile.bladeWidthProfile(-0.5f), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(0f, LandscapeProfile.bladeWidthProfile(1.5f), 1e-6f)
    }

    @Test
    fun sunGlitterIsAUnitGaussianInSlopeSpace() {
        assertEquals(1f, LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(0f, 0f), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(0.3679f, LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(1f, 0f), 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(0.3679f, LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(0f, -1f), 1e-3f)
        // Symmetric in both axes, bounded, and effectively gone at 3 sigma.
        for (dx in listOf(-2f, -0.4f, 0.3f, 1.5f)) {
            for (dy in listOf(-1.2f, 0f, 0.8f)) {
                val g = LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(dx, dy)
                assertTrue("glitter bounded ($dx,$dy)", g in 0f..1f)
                assertEquals(g, LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(-dx, -dy), 1e-6f)
            }
        }
        assertTrue(LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(3f, 0f) < 0.001f)
        assertTrue(
            LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(0.5f, 0f) > LandscapeProfile.sunGlitter(1.5f, 0f)
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun foothillHeightsAreSmoothComparedToTheMassif() {
        val envelope = floatArrayOf(0f, 0.7f, 0.5f, 1f, 1f, 0.5f)
        val foothills = LandscapeProfile.foothillHeights(80, 26f, envelope, seed = 77)
        assertEquals(81, foothills.size)
        var maxStep = 0f
        for (i in 1 until foothills.size) {
            maxStep = maxOf(maxStep, abs(foothills[i] - foothills[i - 1]))
        }
        assertTrue("foothills undulate gently (max step $maxStep)", maxStep < 26f * 0.12f)
        assertTrue(foothills.all { it in 0f..26f })
    }
}
```

## 22. `app/src/test/java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy/ui/persepolis/PersepolisFramingTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.alijafari.red.astronomy.ui.persepolis

import com.alijafari.red.astronomy.domain.SkyCanvasTheme
import com.alijafari.red.astronomy.ui.rendering.PersepolisFraming
import com.alijafari.red.astronomy.ui.rendering.SkySceneLayout
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/**
 * Checks for the combined Persepolis + Mil-e Azhdaha landscape's placement in the Home scene.
 *
 * The ruins' own camera and lighting live in PersepolisMilAzhdaha.kt and need a GL context; what
 * ZIG decides — and what can therefore be proven on the JVM — is where that camera's output lands
 * in the scene: Persepolis at exactly half its first linear size in the left distance and
 * Mil-e Azhdaha alone at the far right, the whole façade on screen, the platform and tower slope
 * in the ground band, the
 * columns against the sky, and no stone over the readout. Those are the assertions below, over a
 * matrix of scene sizes spanning the HomeScreen clamp (52 % of the viewport, 312–560 dp tall)
 * from a small phone to a tablet.
 *
 * Sizes are physical pixels at the density noted per case, because that is what the GL viewport and
 * the sky canvas are both measured in. The full lookAt/ortho projection of landmark points is checked
 * offline by tools/persepolis/verify_framing.py; this test pins the pure-Kotlin framing rules.
 */
class PersepolisFramingTest {

    private data class SceneSize(val widthPx: Float, val heightPx: Float, val density: Float, val label: String)

    private val sizes = listOf(
        SceneSize(960f, 1140f, 3f, "small phone 320x380dp"),
        SceneSize(1080f, 1260f, 3f, "phone 360x420dp"),
        SceneSize(1290f, 1440f, 3f, "large phone 430x480dp"),
        SceneSize(1080f, 936f, 3f, "short phone scene 360x312dp"),
        SceneSize(2400f, 1120f, 2f, "tablet 1200x560dp"),
        SceneSize(2400f, 936f, 3f, "wide short scene 800x312dp")
    )

    private fun framingFor(size: SceneSize): PersepolisFraming.Result {
        val horizonY = size.heightPx * SkySceneLayout.HORIZON_FRACTION
        return PersepolisFraming.forScene(
            widthPx = size.widthPx,
            heightPx = size.heightPx,
            horizonYPx = horizonY,
            readoutBottomPx = horizonY - PersepolisFraming.READOUT_INSET_DP * size.density,
            readoutRightPx = PersepolisFraming.READOUT_RIGHT_DP * size.density,
            readoutClearancePx = PersepolisFraming.READOUT_CLEARANCE_DP * size.density
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun theCombinedLandscapeRetainsExactlyHalfTheFirstLinearSize() {
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            val aspect = size.widthPx / size.heightPx
            // The first integration's framing, recomputed: its model window, widened by the old cap.
            val referenceVisibleWidth = maxOf(
                PersepolisFraming.DEFAULT_VISIBLE_WIDTH_M,
                aspect * PersepolisFraming.BUILDING_HEIGHT_M /
                    PersepolisFraming.REFERENCE_MAX_BUILDING_HEIGHT_FRACTION
            )
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: twice the reference visible width",
                referenceVisibleWidth / PersepolisFraming.LINEAR_SCALE_FRACTION,
                framing.visibleWidthMeters,
                0.01f
            )
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: half the reference pixels per metre",
                0.5f * size.widthPx / referenceVisibleWidth,
                framing.pxPerMeter,
                0.001f
            )
        }
        // On phone-shaped scenes that is concretely the 178 m window (twice the previous 89 m).
        sizes.filter { it.widthPx / it.heightPx < 1.2f }.forEach { size ->
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: half-scale phone window",
                178f,
                framingFor(size).visibleWidthMeters,
                0.001f
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun persepolisStaysLeftAndMilStandsAloneAtTheRight() {
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: centroid on the left third",
                PersepolisFraming.CENTER_FRACTION_FROM_LEFT,
                framing.centerFractionFromLeft,
                0.0001f
            )
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: model centre column",
                size.widthPx * PersepolisFraming.CENTER_FRACTION_FROM_LEFT,
                framing.modelCenterXPx,
                0.5f
            )
            // Persepolis' whole detailed façade remains in frame at the left, exactly as in
            // the previous framing. Its cut-back platform now ends well before the Mil slope:
            // at least a tenth of the scene is ordinary open plain between the two sites.
            assertTrue("${size.label}: façade left in frame", framing.facadeLeftXPx >= 0f)
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: façade confined to the left (right=${framing.facadeRightXPx / size.widthPx})",
                framing.facadeRightXPx <= size.widthPx * 0.56f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: cut-back platform unchanged (right=${framing.platformRightXPx / size.widthPx})",
                framing.platformRightXPx <= size.widthPx * 0.56f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: Mil slope separated by open plain " +
                    "(platform=${framing.platformRightXPx / size.widthPx}, " +
                    "slope=${framing.milSlopeLeftXPx / size.widthPx})",
                framing.milSlopeLeftXPx > framing.platformRightXPx + size.widthPx * 0.10f &&
                    framing.milTowerCenterXPx > size.widthPx * 0.58f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: combined footprint ordering",
                framing.platformLeftXPx < framing.facadeLeftXPx &&
                    framing.facadeLeftXPx < framing.modelCenterXPx &&
                    framing.modelCenterXPx < framing.milSlopeLeftXPx &&
                    framing.milSlopeLeftXPx < framing.milTowerCenterXPx &&
                    framing.milTowerCenterXPx < framing.milSlopeRightXPx &&
                    framing.facadeRightXPx < framing.platformRightXPx &&
                    framing.platformRightXPx < framing.milSlopeLeftXPx &&
                    framing.platformRightXPx < framing.milSlopeRightXPx
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun theMilTowerAndSlopeUseTheirProjectedFootprint() {
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            // The Mil group is much nearer the fixed camera than the Persepolis toe, so these
            // fields intentionally use precomputed view-space offsets rather than raw world x/y.
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: tower centre from its view offset",
                framing.modelCenterXPx +
                    PersepolisFraming.MIL_TOWER_CENTER_VIEW_OFFSET_M * framing.pxPerMeter,
                framing.milTowerCenterXPx,
                0.5f
            )
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: slope left from its view offset",
                framing.modelCenterXPx +
                    PersepolisFraming.MIL_SLOPE_LEFT_VIEW_OFFSET_M * framing.pxPerMeter,
                framing.milSlopeLeftXPx,
                0.5f
            )
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: slope right from its view offset",
                framing.modelCenterXPx +
                    PersepolisFraming.MIL_SLOPE_RIGHT_VIEW_OFFSET_M * framing.pxPerMeter,
                framing.milSlopeRightXPx,
                0.5f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: tower rises from the slope without reaching the header",
                framing.tallestColumnTopYPx < framing.milTowerTopYPx &&
                    framing.milTowerTopYPx < framing.platformTopYPx &&
                    framing.milTowerTopYPx > size.heightPx * 0.36f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: slope is grounded below its top and in front of the toe",
                framing.milSlopeTopYPx < framing.toeYPx &&
                    framing.toeYPx < framing.milSlopeFrontYPx &&
                    framing.milSlopeFrontYPx <= framing.toeYPx + size.heightPx * 0.027f
            )
        }

        // The canonical phone composition: Persepolis left, open plain, then the tower at
        // the far right with its upslope running modestly beyond the edge of the frame.
        val phone = framingFor(sizes.first { it.label == "phone 360x420dp" })
        assertEquals(0.908f, phone.milTowerCenterXPx / 1080f, 0.002f)
        assertEquals(0.763f, phone.milSlopeLeftXPx / 1080f, 0.002f)
        assertEquals(1.098f, phone.milSlopeRightXPx / 1080f, 0.002f)
    }

    @Test
    fun theToeSitsInTheGroundBandWithForegroundBelowIt() {
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            val composition = SkySceneLayout.composition(size.widthPx, size.heightPx)
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: toe below the solid ground's top",
                framing.toeYPx > composition.groundTopY
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: toe is farther back, with a deeper foreground below it",
                framing.toeYPx >= composition.horizonY + size.heightPx * 0.095f &&
                    framing.toeYPx <= size.heightPx * 0.86f &&
                    framing.toeYPx <= size.heightPx * PersepolisFraming.MAX_TOE_FRACTION + 0.5f &&
                    framing.toeYPx < size.heightPx
            )
            assertEquals(
                "${size.label}: base fraction is the toe row",
                framing.toeYPx / size.heightPx,
                framing.baseFractionFromTop,
                0.0001f
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun thePlatformAndSlopeAreInTheGroundAndTheColumnsAreAgainstTheSky() {
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            val horizonY = size.heightPx * SkySceneLayout.HORIZON_FRACTION
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: platform top below the horizon",
                framing.platformTopYPx > horizonY
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: Mil slope top below the horizon",
                framing.milSlopeTopYPx > horizonY
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: tallest column rises above the horizon (top=${framing.tallestColumnTopYPx}, horizon=$horizonY)",
                framing.tallestColumnTopYPx < horizonY
            )
            // And never up into the header band (the top scrim is 0.34 of the scene; the brand and
            // actions live above roughly the first quarter).
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: tallest column clears the header band",
                framing.tallestColumnTopYPx > size.heightPx * 0.36f
            )
            val buildingFraction = (framing.toeYPx - framing.tallestColumnTopYPx) / size.heightPx
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: building height capped ($buildingFraction)",
                buildingFraction <= PersepolisFraming.MAX_BUILDING_HEIGHT_FRACTION + 0.001f
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun noStoneReachesTheReadout() {
        // The GL surface composites above the Compose readout inside its bounds. Moving the
        // combined group left puts more than the great doorway under the caption, so the guard
        // is the tallest Persepolis structure whose projected span actually enters the protected
        // readout width at this size. The lower, rightward tower is checked conditionally here
        // and by exact projection in the Python verifier.
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            val readoutBottom = size.heightPx * SkySceneLayout.HORIZON_FRACTION -
                PersepolisFraming.READOUT_INSET_DP * size.density
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: guarded structure (${framing.readoutGuardTopYPx}) below readout bottom ($readoutBottom)",
                framing.readoutGuardTopYPx >= readoutBottom +
                    PersepolisFraming.READOUT_CLEARANCE_DP * size.density - 0.5f
            )
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: doorway cornice also below readout bottom",
                framing.greatDoorwayTopYPx >= readoutBottom +
                    PersepolisFraming.READOUT_CLEARANCE_DP * size.density - 0.5f
            )
            if (framing.milTowerCenterXPx < PersepolisFraming.READOUT_RIGHT_DP * size.density) {
                assertTrue(
                    "${size.label}: Mil tower also below readout bottom when it enters its width",
                    framing.milTowerTopYPx >= readoutBottom +
                        PersepolisFraming.READOUT_CLEARANCE_DP * size.density - 0.5f
                )
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun theScenesOwnAutoFitNeverWidensTheChosenWidth() {
        // PersepolisMilAzhdahaScene.draw() widens when aspect * 25 / baseFraction exceeds the chosen
        // width. That must never trigger, or the scale the ground painter assumes would not be the
        // scale the GL camera uses and the grounding seam would drift from the stone.
        sizes.forEach { size ->
            val framing = framingFor(size)
            val aspect = size.widthPx / size.heightPx
            val autoFitNeed = aspect * (25f / framing.baseFractionFromTop)
            assertTrue(
                "${size.label}: chosen width ${framing.visibleWidthMeters} >= auto-fit need $autoFitNeed",
                framing.visibleWidthMeters >= autoFitNeed
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun onlyThePhotographicThemesCarryTheRuins() {
        assertTrue(PersepolisFeature.isEnabledFor(SkyCanvasTheme.REAL_SKY))
        assertTrue(PersepolisFeature.isEnabledFor(SkyCanvasTheme.ATMOSPHERIC_SKY))
        // The flat art-directed themes — and Observatory, whose red output is functional — keep
        // their existing ground exactly as it was.
        assertFalse(PersepolisFeature.isEnabledFor(SkyCanvasTheme.MONOCHROME_SCIENTIFIC))
        assertFalse(PersepolisFeature.isEnabledFor(SkyCanvasTheme.KIDS_WATERCOLOR))
        assertFalse(PersepolisFeature.isEnabledFor(SkyCanvasTheme.OBSERVATORY))
        assertFalse(PersepolisFeature.isEnabledFor(SkyCanvasTheme.PAPERCRAFT_DIORAMA))
    }

    @Test
    fun moonlightScalesWithTheIlluminatedFraction() {
        assertEquals(0.22f, persepolisMoonIntensity(100.0), 0.0001f)
        assertEquals(0.11f, persepolisMoonIntensity(50.0), 0.0001f)
        assertEquals(0f, persepolisMoonIntensity(0.0), 0.0001f)
        // Out-of-range engine values can never overdrive the model's full-Moon intensity.
        assertEquals(0.22f, persepolisMoonIntensity(140.0), 0.0001f)
        assertEquals(0f, persepolisMoonIntensity(-5.0), 0.0001f)
    }
}
```

## 23. `app/src/test/java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy/ui/persepolis/PersepolisHostingGuardTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.alijafari.red.astronomy.ui.persepolis

import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

/**
 * Source-shape guards for the Persepolis + Mil-e Azhdaha hosting (not GL tests — the
 * relief needs a GL context, which the JVM does not have).
 *
 * The ruins are hosted on a TextureView ([PersepolisTextureView]) so they composite
 * into the window like the painted layers around them: Compose siblings drawn later
 * (the search overlay, the readout, dialogs-in-window) cover and dim them, and they
 * scroll with the Home feed. The previous window-level overlay surface claimed the top
 * window z-order, which let the relief punch through every Compose overlay on screen
 * — the search window most visibly. These guards keep that regression from returning
 * silently.
 */
class PersepolisHostingGuardTest {

    @Test
    fun reliefIsHostedByATextureViewWithItsOwnEglThread() {
        val host = readMain("java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy/ui/persepolis/PersepolisTextureView.kt")
        assertTrue("the relief host must be a TextureView", host.contains(": TextureView("))
        assertTrue("the host must own an EGL thread driving the scene", host.contains("EGL14"))
        assertTrue("the host must present frames itself", host.contains("eglSwapBuffers"))
        assertTrue("the host must render the unchanged scene", host.contains("PersepolisMilAzhdahaScene"))
    }

    @Test
    fun noWindowTopSurfaceRemainsInThePersepolisPackage() {
        val pkg = File(mainDir(), "java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy/ui/persepolis")
        assertTrue("persepolis package missing", pkg.isDirectory)
        val files = pkg.listFiles { f -> f.isFile && f.extension == "kt" }.orEmpty()
        assertTrue("persepolis package has no Kotlin sources", files.isNotEmpty())
        for (file in files) {
            val text = file.readText()
            assertFalse(
                "${file.name} must not host the relief on a framework GL surface view",
                text.contains("GLSurfaceView")
            )
            assertFalse(
                "${file.name} must not claim the top window z-order (that is what punched through the search overlay)",
                text.contains("setZOrderOnTop")
            )
        }
    }

    private fun readMain(relative: String): String {
        val f = File(mainDir(), relative)
        assertTrue("missing source file $relative", f.isFile)
        return f.readText()
    }

    private fun mainDir(): File {
        var dir: File? = File("").absoluteFile
        while (dir != null) {
            val candidate = File(dir, "app/src/main")
            if (File(candidate, "java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy").isDirectory) return candidate
            val direct = File(dir, "src/main")
            if (File(direct, "java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy").isDirectory) return direct
            dir = dir.parentFile
        }
        throw AssertionError("could not locate the app main source set")
    }
}
```

## 24. `app/src/test/java/com/alijafari/red/astronomy/ui/rendering/CloudVeilRendererTest.kt`

```kotlin
package com.alijafari.red.astronomy.ui.rendering

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/**
 * Contract for the cloud veils' deterministic hash.
 *
 * Regression guard for the white band across the Home scene: the hash masks 31 bits,
 * so it must be scaled by 2^31 − 1. At 2^24 − 1 it returned 0..128 — the Y jitter
 * threw veils far off-canvas, the alpha factor ran up to ~32×, and one cirrus streak
 * landed just above the horizon, where the scene-motion clip at the horizon line
 * sliced it into a flat-topped glowing rectangle over the mountains.
 */
class CloudVeilRendererTest {

    @Test
    fun pseudoRandomStaysInUnitRangeAndIsDeterministic() {
        // Every seed the veil layers actually use (layer/ instance grids), and beyond.
        for (seed in -50..600) {
            val value = CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(seed)
            assertTrue("pseudoRandom in 0..1 (seed=$seed): $value", value in 0f..1f)
            assertEquals(value, CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(seed), 0f)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun cirrusStreakThatFormedTheBandIsBackInItsLayer() {
        // Layer 0, instance 1 — the streak that formed the band. Its three hashes are
        // seeded at 37 (x), 17 (y jitter) and 53 (alpha); all were ≫ 1 before the fix:
        // y jitter 29.8 threw the sprite ~830 px down to the horizon, and the alpha
        // factor 23.5 drew it at 0.75 alpha instead of the intended ~0.03.
        assertTrue(CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(37) in 0f..1f)
        assertTrue(CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(17) in 0f..1f)
        assertTrue(CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(53) in 0f..1f)
        val alphaFactor = 0.75f + 0.25f * CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(53)
        assertTrue("alpha factor in 0.75..1: $alphaFactor", alphaFactor in 0.75f..1f)
        val yJitterFraction = (CloudVeilRenderer.pseudoRandom(17) - 0.5f) * 0.02f
        assertTrue("y jitter within ±1% of scene height: $yJitterFraction", yJitterFraction in -0.01f..0.01f)
    }
}
```
